Dear Members of the Evaluation Committee:
Northern Essex serves more than 5,000 students across Haverhill and Lawrence, and as the first federally designated Hispanic Serving Institution in New England, your mission sits at the center of the Merrimack Valley. The challenge you named in this RFP is specific: attract new prospects, convert the prospects who apply, and do it inside a tight, defined service territory. That is the exact work we do every day for community colleges.
Feel free to view this proposal video link giving context to our overall submission:
https://shorturl.at/6QGBt
Scan To View Proposal Video
.
Propellant Media is an Atlanta-based digital marketing agency founded in 2015, with a second office in Charlotte. We are a certified Minority Business Enterprise (NMSDC) and a Google Premier Partner, which places us in the top 3% of agencies in the United States. We run enrollment campaigns for community colleges, public universities, and graduate programs, and we manage the full channel mix this RFP calls for: paid search, paid social, programmatic display, video, connected TV, streaming audio, audience and list-based retargeting, and geofencing.
A few reasons we believe we are the right partner for Northern Essex:
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Live Slate Experience - We work with a number of universities and colleges mapping Slate to full attribution data via digital advertising platforms.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We know the community college funnel — We market two-year and certificate enrollment for a living — our flagship community college client grew Fall degree-seeking enrollment 63.2% year over year.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We serve a comparable HSI audience — Our work at the Community College of Aurora, a fellow Hispanic Serving Institution, mirrors your 49% Latino and first-generation audience.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We geofence to your exact territory — We will build campaigns around your 22 cities and towns and feeder high schools, not a generic regional radius.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We plug into your existing accounts — We work inside your Google Analytics, Google Ads, and Meta accounts — you keep ownership of the data.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We give you live dashboard access — Near real-time visibility into spend, pacing, traffic, and conversions, with monthly analysis and recommendations.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We act as a partner, not a vendor — Proactive channel recommendations, audience analysis, and untapped-market discovery — not just execution against a list of deliverables.
Propellant Media has reviewed the requirements of NECC26-MKT01 in full and submits this proposal in compliance with its terms. Our pricing is valid for at least ninety (90) days from the bid closing date, and we are prepared to sign the Commonwealth of Massachusetts Standard Contract within five (5) days of an award.
Finally, below are sample creative ads our team developed.  We understand your team may have an in-house creative department, and we wanted to showcase how we can partner in that capacity.
View Creative Link Here - https://shorturl.at/JmBVU
Thank you for the opportunity. We would welcome the chance to present our approach to your committee during the week of July 6.
Respectfully submitted,
_________________________________
Justin Croxton
Chief Executive Officer, Propellant Media, LLC
800 Battery Avenue SE, Suite 300, Atlanta, GA 30339
1 (877) 776-7358  |  justin@propellant.media
Northern Essex is competing for prospective students inside one of the most defined geographies in this RFP process: 22 cities and towns across upper Essex County, plus 16 New Hampshire border zip codes where your prime audience lives. Over 95% of your students live within 20 minutes of the Haverhill and Lawrence campuses. That concentration is an advantage — it lets us spend media where it matters and waste almost none of it.
Below is what we heard in your RFP, mapped to how we will respond. This is the spine of our proposal.

Why Northern Essex, and Why Now
We did not write this for a generic community college. We wrote it for a Hispanic Serving Institution recruiting first-generation and Latino students inside a 20-minute commuter radius, against four-year transfer destinations with bigger budgets. We have run that exact funnel.
•         We have run this exact funnel. Our flagship community college client — a fellow Hispanic Serving Institution — grew Fall degree-seeking enrollment 63.2% year over year and generated 1,381 qualified leads in a single 30-day window. That is the same two-year, first-generation, Latino-heavy funnel Northern Essex recruits into every cycle.
•         We know the Merrimack Valley competition. Your prospects weigh NECC against four-year transfer destinations, online competitors, and the option not to enroll at all. For in-territory Massachusetts audiences, the tuition- and fee-free MassEducate and MassReconnect message beats every one of them — and we lead with it.
•         The New Hampshire border is open whitespace. Sixteen New Hampshire zips sit in your prime zone but outside your “official” Massachusetts territory. They are an underexploited paid-search and geofencing target, and we work with them with the same channel mix and unified, program-eligibility messaging — not state-specific tuition language that would confuse the audience.
Our Year-One Commitment
In the first eleven months, we will stand up seasonal enrollment campaigns for fall, winter, spring, and summer, support your event and program-specific initiatives, and refine targeting continuously against live conversion data. NECC has set a credit enrollment goal of 6,000 students for FY2026-2027 — up from 5,000+ today — and every dollar we spend will be measured against the inquiries, applications, and enrollments that close that gap. Through MassEducate and MassReconnect, many eligible Massachusetts students can attend tuition- and fee-free. That is not a footnote. It is the headline of every awareness campaign we run.

We do both. The result is a plan you can evaluate as a working strategy, not a brochure.
Every item below maps to the scope language in your RFP. Where a service falls outside our core, we say so plainly and frame it honestly.

Propellant Media was founded in 2015 and is headquartered at 800 Battery Avenue SE, Suite 300, Atlanta, GA 30339, with a second office in Charlotte, North Carolina. We are a Google Premier Partner — the top 3% of U.S. agencies — and a certified Minority Business Enterprise through the NMSDC. Higher education enrollment marketing is a core practice, not a side line.
Over a decade in, we have built the agency around a single discipline: turning paid media into measurable enrollment outcomes for institutions that have to account for every dollar. We are large enough to run a full omnichannel program and small enough that a named team — not a rotating pod — runs your account every cycle.
What We Do
We manage the full digital channel mix this RFP calls for, all in-house:
•         Paid search (SEM) — Google Search, Display, and remarketing, structured by program intent and tuned continuously against cost per inquiry.
•         Paid social — Meta (Facebook/Instagram), with audience segmentation, creative testing, and platform expansion where the audience data supports it.
•         Programmatic & video — Programmatic display, connected TV, and streaming audio for territory-specific awareness.
•         Geofencing & location targeting — Campus, feeder-high-school, and competitor-location targeting down to the zip code.
•         Retargeting — Site-visitor and list-based retargeting to move prospects from interest to application.
•         Analytics, attribution & CRM integration — GA4, conversion tracking, live dashboards, and integration into CRMs including Slate, Salesforce, and TargetX.
•         Landing page & conversion optimization — Conversion advisory that lifts the inquiry and application rates your paid traffic produces.
The Institution Types We Serve
We work across the institution types that matter for this RFP — community colleges, public universities, graduate programs, and health-sciences programs. A selection of our higher education portfolio:

How We Work
The operating model is built for institutions, not retail clients:
•         We work inside your accounts. Campaigns run in NECC’s own Google Ads, Meta, and Google Analytics environments. You keep ownership of the accounts, the data, and the audience history — even if the partnership ever ends.
•         Working media is your money. We separate working media from our management fee on every invoice. Unspent media stays yours; it is never absorbed as agency margin.
•         A named team owns the account. You get the same account director, analytics lead, and media specialists every cycle — people who learn your funnel and compound on it rather than relearning it each year.
•         We report against outcomes, not impressions. Every dashboard and every monthly review ties spend to inquiries, applications, and enrollment — the metrics that close an enrollment gap.
Why Higher Education Institutions Choose Us
•         A community college track record, not a four-year playbook applied downward. Our flagship community college and fellow HSI grew Fall degree-seeking enrollment 63.2% year over year and produced 1,381 qualified leads in a single 30-day window — the same first-generation, Latino, adult-learner funnel Northern Essex recruits into.
•         Cost discipline proven at community-college budgets. At AB Tech, a defined-budget community college much like NECC, we delivered 598 qualified leads in 90 days at a $20.69 cost per lead — strong output from a modest media spend.
•         Staying power. We have managed Kennesaw State’s undergraduate paid search since 2019 — a partnership now in its sixth-plus year at a $7.98 cost per lead and a 12.32% conversion rate. Consistency across recruitment cycles is itself the proof.
•         Credentials that match your mission. As an NMSDC-certified Minority Business Enterprise, we are aligned with the equity and access mission of a Hispanic Serving Institution — not just claiming it.
These are the real people who will run Northern Essex — not generic titles. Alyssa Dixon is your day-to-day lead and owns your weekly call.

<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="ffffff" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000102"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Monthly (or cadence NECC prefers)
A strong launch is not a switch you flip — it is four deliberate weeks that decide how well every campaign performs afterward. Below is exactly how we stand up Northern Essex’s program: what happens in each of the first four weeks, the ongoing rhythm that keeps spend efficient once campaigns are live, and the monthly reconciliation that keeps your budget transparent. By the end of Week 4 your campaigns are live, your dashboard is open, and we have a baseline to optimize against — no guesswork, no lost month.
Campaign Launch (Weeks 1–4)
•         Week 1 — Access & discovery — We connect to your Google Analytics, Google Ads, Meta, and GTM environments, confirm conversion tracking, and begin scoping Slate CRM integration — timed to NECC’s Summer/Fall 2026 implementation — so lead data flows into Slate from day one.
•         Week 2 — Audience & geo build — We build audiences off your Exhibit A territory, feeder high schools, and prospect lists, and map the seasonal calendar.
•         Week 3 — Campaign construction — Search, social, programmatic, and geofencing campaigns built and QA’d; tracking validated end to end.
•         Week 4 — Launch & baseline — Campaigns go live, dashboard access is granted, and we establish performance baselines for the first optimization cycle.
Ongoing Management Cadence

Budget Reconciliation
Northern Essex requires monthly invoicing with a clear, separate breakout of advertising spend versus management fees. We invoice exactly that way — every invoice lists dates of service, services provided, a detailed budget breakdown, working media versus management fee, and the purchase order number, sent to the two accounts payable addresses specified in your RFP. Platform accounts remain in NECC’s ownership throughout the engagement — media is managed within your accounts, not ours.

If approved media spend is not fully deployed in a given month, the unspent balance stays available to
NECC — it is never absorbed as agency margin. We reconcile spend to plan every month so there are no surprises.
Below is an example of the budget reconciliation we oversee for a large public university.
Your RFP asks several direct questions about reporting. We answer each one here.
The Dashboard We Provide
We provide a live, user-accessible online dashboard giving NECC near real-time visibility into campaign performance. You and your team get direct, continuous access — not a monthly PDF you wait on. Access levels are configurable (view-only for stakeholders, full access for your marketing leads), and data refreshes daily.

<w:trPr><w:cantSplit w:val="1"/><w:tblHeader w:val="0"/></w:trPr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="f5f5f5" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000013D"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Efficiency
<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="f5f5f5" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000013E"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Cost per lead, cost per application
<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="f5f5f5" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000013F"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Measures working-media efficiency month over month
How NECC defines success in the dashboard: Qualified traffic means visitors demonstrating meaningful engagement — landing page time, program page clicks, inquiry form submissions, and application clicks. Awareness is measured through reach, impressions, website traffic, branded search activity, and video engagement. Every KPI in the dashboard maps to one of these definitions.
Sample Dashboard
</w:sdtPr><w:sdtContent><w:tbl><w:tblPr><w:tblStyle w:val="Table12"/><w:tblW w:w="9350.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:jc w:val="left"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:insideH w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:insideV w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/></w:tblBorders><w:tblLayout w:type="fixed"/><w:tblLook w:val="0400"/></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="1898"/><w:gridCol w:w="7452"/><w:tblGridChange w:id="0"><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="1898"/><w:gridCol w:w="7452"/></w:tblGrid></w:tblGridChange></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:trPr><w:cantSplit w:val="1"/><w:tblHeader w:val="0"/></w:trPr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="e63412" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000143"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="ffffff"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Live CRM & Dashboard Walkthrough
<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000144"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">A live CRM and attribution walkthrough video is available in our finalist presentation that shows how your CRM data pipes into the dashboard you’d have 24/7 access to
Your RFP requires campaigns to be incorporated into NECC's existing Google Analytics, Google Ads, and Meta accounts for tracking. We build on that foundation. Your accounts and your data stay yours; we connect the advertising layer to your system of record so every dollar can be traced to an outcome.
How We Connect the Funnel
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Step 1 — Pixel-to-Ping Data Connection — We place and validate conversion tracking across Google Ads, Meta, and programmatic so every inquiry and application is captured at the source.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Step 2 — Audience Mirroring — Prospect and inquiry data feeds back into audience targeting, so spend concentrates on the segments most likely to apply and enroll.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Step 3 — Application Yield Reporting — We report not just leads, but how leads progress to applications — the conversion problem you named in this RFP.
Slate CRM Integration
Slate implementation — Northern Essex is implementing Slate in Summer/Fall 2026. We begin integration scoping in onboarding so campaign leads feed directly into Slate records — enabling closed-loop reporting from ad impression to enrolled student.
CRM list activation — NECC-provided audience lists (prospects, lapsed inquiries, current students, alumni) in CSV/Excel format activated across Google Customer Match, Meta Custom Audiences, and programmatic platforms — fully FERPA compliant.
Integration method — API, webhook, or CSV approach scoped with NECC's IT and enrollment teams during onboarding based on Slate implementation status.
Segmented Audiences & Predictive Targeting
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Geography-first segmentation — Audiences are built around your 22 towns, NH border zips, and feeder high schools.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Audience-type segmentation — Traditional-age students, adult learners, career changers, and first-generation/Latino prospects targeted with tailored messaging.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Behavioral retargeting — Site visitors and form-starters are re-engaged with sequenced creative to drive application completion.
<w:tblPr><w:tblStyle w:val="Table13"/><w:tblW w:w="9350.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:jc w:val="left"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:insideH w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:insideV w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/></w:tblBorders><w:tblLayout w:type="fixed"/><w:tblLook w:val="0400"/></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="1898"/><w:gridCol w:w="7452"/><w:tblGridChange w:id="0"><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="1898"/><w:gridCol w:w="7452"/></w:tblGrid></w:tblGridChange></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:trPr><w:cantSplit w:val="1"/><w:tblHeader w:val="0"/></w:trPr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="e63412" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000156"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="ffffff"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">AUTOMATION
<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000157"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We use BigQuery, Google Sheets, and Zapier to move data between your ad accounts, analytics, and reporting dashboard automatically — so reporting reflects reality without manual exports. If NECC adopts a dedicated CRM (Slate, Salesforce, TargetX, or Element451) during the term, we connect natively or via API at no change to your management fee.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Your RFP lists landing page and conversion recommendations in scope. We do not redesign your website — your team owns that. What we do is fix the specific places where paid traffic leaks out of the funnel before it converts. Most of the campaigns we run for community colleges lose more prospects on the landing page and the application form than they ever lose in the ad auction. That is where the cheapest enrollment gains live, and it is exactly the conversion problem NECC named in this RFP.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We focus on the four friction points you described: financial concern that surfaces too late on the page, process uncertainty in the enrollment steps, application abandonment at form start, and mobile friction that costs conversions before a student ever submits.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="180" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">How We Work the Conversion Problem
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Optimization is a loop, not a one-time audit. We diagnose where prospects drop, test changes against a control, measure the lift in real completions, and scale what works. The graphic below shows the cycle and the four problems we solve for Northern Essex.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="180" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">What We Do
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Conversion-focused landing pages — We recommend dedicated pages for high-priority programs and seasonal campaigns, built to convert paid traffic rather than serve as general information pages. A prospect who clicks a fall-nursing ad should land on a fall-nursing page with one clear next step — not the homepage.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Form and CTA testing — We test form length, field count, and call-to-action placement to lift inquiry and RFI completion rates. Every removed field and every clearer button is measured against a control, so we know the change actually moved completions.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Mobile and page-speed review — Most prospective students arrive on mobile; we flag speed and usability issues that quietly cost conversions. A page that loads two seconds slower on a phone can lose a meaningful share of inquiries before the form even renders.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Conversion benchmarks — We set realistic benchmarks by program and channel so improvement is measurable, not anecdotal. “Better” is not a number; a 14% form-completion rate moving to 19% is.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="180" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Where the Lift Comes From
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Lead with eligibility, not credentials. For in-territory Massachusetts audiences, the tuition- and fee-free MassEducate and MassReconnect message belongs above the fold — the single most powerful objection-remover a community college landing page can carry.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Shorten the path to inquiry. Fewer form fields, a visible next step, and a clear CTA on every program page convert more of the traffic you are already paying for.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Win the mobile-first prospect. Speed, tap-target sizing, and a form that works one-handed protect the conversions that desktop-built pages quietly lose.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="180" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">What We Don't Touch
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We are deliberate about scope. We do not rebuild your CMS, re-architect your site, or take ownership of pages your team maintains. Our role is the advisory and testing layer that sits on top of the site you already run — recommendations, A/B tests, and benchmarks that your web team implements or that we build as dedicated campaign landing pages where that is faster. You keep control of the brand and the platform; we make the pages that receive paid traffic convert.
Optimization is where a campaign is won or lost. Anyone can launch ads; the difference shows up in what happens next — whether budget keeps flowing toward what converts or sits in channels that have stopped working. We optimize daily, run a structured pass every week, and never wait for a quarterly reset to fix a problem we can see today. Every reallocation decision follows NECC’s confirmed KPI hierarchy: inquiries and applications first, qualified traffic second, brand awareness third.
The diagram below shows how a single reallocation decision actually gets made — and the priority order that governs it.

Our Optimization Discipline
•         Daily eyes, weekly action. We check pacing and anomalies every day and run a full optimization pass weekly — search terms, audiences, creative rotation, and budget reallocation — so problems are caught in days, not months.
•         Money follows performance. Budget moves toward the programs, towns, and channels producing the lowest cost per inquiry, and away from whatever has stopped converting — within the same month, not the next contract cycle.
•         Test, don’t guess. Every meaningful change is measured against a control so we can prove a lift, keep what works, and reverse what doesn’t.

<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000187"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">At a community college client, mid-campaign data showed Meta driving lower-cost inquiries than display for adult learners. We shifted budget into Meta within the same month, and the campaign produced 759 Meta-sourced leads inside a 30-day window — part of 1,381 total leads. The lesson: we move money toward what is converting, fast, instead of waiting for the next quarter.
The Competitive Landscape for Northern Essex Community College
Northern Essex competes on two fronts: against nearby four-year institutions for traditional-age students, and against other access-oriented options for adult learners and career changers. Your edge is proximity, affordability, and a clear HSI identity. The plan below is built to press that edge.
The sharper truth is that Northern Essex's toughest competitor often isn't another school at all — it's the decision to not enroll. For a working adult weighing a career change or a first-generation student unsure college is for them, the alternative to NECC is staying put. That reframes the job: against four-year institutions, we win on the tuition- and fee-free MassEducate and MassReconnect message that no university or career college can match; against other community colleges and online options, we win on proximity, since over 95% of your students already live within 20 minutes of Haverhill or Lawrence; and against inertia, we win by making the path feel achievable — bilingual, equity-aligned creative and a clear next step that turns "maybe someday" into an application. Each tactic in the plan below maps to one of those three fights.

<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Brand visibility — community colleges compete for attention against four-year brands with larger budgets.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Conversion gap — the RFP names difficulty converting applicants, a funnel-stage problem to solve directly.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•No housing — a commuter-only footprint limits reach to the immediate territory.

<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•NH border audience — 16 New Hampshire zips in your prime zone are an underexploited paid-search and geofencing target.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Bilingual creative — a ~49% Latino population rewards Spanish-language and culturally relevant messaging.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Adult learners & workforce — career-changer and workforce-aligned programs are high-intent, lower-competition segments.
<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="000001AB"><w:pPr><w:spacing w:after="80" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e65100"/><w:sz w:val="21"/><w:szCs w:val="21"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">THREATS
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Four-year competition — nearby universities advertising aggressively for the same traditional-age students.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Rising CPMs — digital media inflation in the education category erodes efficiency without active optimization.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="792"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="60" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="792" w:hanging="288"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•Demographic headwinds — a shrinking pool of traditional-age students in the Northeast raises the value of every lead.
Competitive positioning observations: NECC's geographic concentration is rare and valuable; the NH border zips are the clearest paid whitespace; and bilingual creative is both a mission fit and a competitive advantage few peers execute well. MassEducate and MassReconnect make Northern Essex tuition- and fee-free for a significant portion of eligible Massachusetts students — a value proposition no four-year institution, online competitor, or career college can match.
<w:pgSz w:h="15840" w:w="12240" w:orient="portrait"/><w:pgMar w:bottom="1440" w:top="1440" w:left="1440" w:right="1440" w:header="720" w:footer="720"/></w:sectPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Our campaigns lead with this for in-territory Massachusetts audiences. A second competitive dynamic worth naming: for some prospects, the real competition is not another institution — it is the decision not to enroll at all. Messaging that makes the process feel accessible and achievable for working adults and first-generation families addresses this directly.
Media Buying & Media Plan
Northern Essex's challenge is precise: attract new prospects and convert the ones who apply, inside a service territory of 22 cities and towns where nearly all of your students already live. Our approach is to spend where your prospects are — geofencing campuses and feeder high schools, layering paid search for high-intent program seekers, and using paid social to build awareness among adult learners and first-generation families — then move budget continuously toward whatever is converting applications.
Our media philosophy is full-funnel and data-led. At the top, programmatic display, video, connected TV, and streaming audio build awareness within territory. In the middle, paid social and retargeting nurture interest and pull prospects toward an inquiry. At the bottom, paid search and list-based retargeting capture and convert high-intent demand into applications. Every stage feeds the next, and every dollar is measured against inquiries and applications, not vanity metrics.
Given a Year-One budget of $130,000 to $140,000 inclusive of media, we recommend concentrating working media on the channels that convert community college prospects: paid search and paid social as the workhorses, geofencing and programmatic for territory-specific reach, and a measured CTV/streaming layer for awareness. The allocation below reflects that priority and is built to flex by season — heavier in the fall and spring enrollment pushes, lighter in between.
The below graphic forms the framework in how Propellant Media executes media planning.
Recommended Channel Allocation
Allocation shown as a percentage of working media. Final dollar figures appear in the Pricing & Budget Proposal section.

<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="ffffff" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="50.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="000001D1"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="19"/><w:szCs w:val="19"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Re-engage prospect & inquiry lists
Channel allocation reflects NECC's historical performance: paid search has been the strongest driver of inquiries and applications — capturing high-intent students actively researching programs. Meta has delivered consistent awareness, engagement, and lead activity. The remaining channels — geofencing, programmatic, CTV, and audio — extend reach within your territory and nurture prospects earlier in the funnel. The full budget covers all four seasonal enrollment cycles plus event-specific campaigns as they arise; event campaigns are not funded separately, and any unspent media rolls forward rather than being absorbed.
Application Projections
Projections are directional and based on community college benchmark cost-per-lead and application-conversion rates. They are provided to illustrate scale, not as guaranteed outcomes.
The ranges below assume the recommended channel mix running across all four enrollment cycles, with budget shifting toward the programs and towns that convert most efficiently as live data comes in. The conservative scenario reflects a cautious first-year ramp while we build NECC-specific performance history; the target scenario reflects steady-state performance once campaigns are optimized; and the aggressive scenario assumes strong creative and a responsive landing-page experience working in our favor. As real conversion data accrues, we update these figures every cycle so planning is based on your funnel, not an industry average.

Campaign Cycle Timing
<w:pgSz w:h="15840" w:w="12240" w:orient="portrait"/><w:pgMar w:bottom="1440" w:top="1440" w:left="1440" w:right="1440" w:header="720" w:footer="720"/></w:sectPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Media flights align to your four enrollment cycles — fall, winter, spring, and summer — with awareness building 8–10 weeks ahead of each registration window and conversion pressure peaking as deadlines approach. New Hampshire border zip campaigns run the same channel mix and creative strategy as Massachusetts — NECC's unified messaging emphasizes broad program eligibility rather than state-specific tuition language, with geofencing concentrated in the 16 NH towns where NECC-bound prospects live.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Forward strategy is not a buzzword section. Northern Essex has a defined budget and a defined territory, and AI matters here for one reason: it makes that budget work harder — finding the right prospects faster, refreshing creative automatically, and opening new low-cost surfaces before competitors get there. Below is specifically how we put these tools to work, organized into three areas: smarter targeting, smarter creative, and the new AI-powered surfaces now opening up. A human owns every decision throughout.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Where AI Makes NECC’s Budget Work Harder
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="160" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Smarter Targeting
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         AI-powered audience modeling — We use machine-learning audience signals in Google and Meta to find prospects who resemble your best applicants, not just broad demographics.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Predictive targeting — Conversion data trains bidding toward the segments and times most likely to produce applications, reducing wasted spend.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Performance Max — Google’s AI-driven campaign type extends reach across Search, Display, YouTube, and Gmail while protecting cost-per-application targets.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Anonymous visitor identification — Where compliant, we identify and retarget high-intent site visitors who did not complete an inquiry.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="160" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Smarter Creative
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Dynamic Creative Optimization (DCO) — Headlines, images, and CTAs are tested and assembled automatically so the best-performing combinations serve more often — useful when NECC’s in-house team supplies the raw assets and we make them perform across formats.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         Creative fatigue monitoring — AI flags when an ad’s performance starts to decay so we rotate before it drags down efficiency, not after.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:before="160" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="e63412"/><w:sz w:val="22"/><w:szCs w:val="22"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">New AI-Powered Surfaces
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="120" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Search and discovery are shifting from blue links to AI-generated answers, and a new advertising surface has opened alongside them. We help Northern Essex get there early, at low cost, without betting core budget on an unproven channel.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         ChatGPT Ads — OpenAI opened self-serve advertising inside ChatGPT in 2026, with contextual targeting and no minimum spend. It reaches a large base of users actively researching decisions — exactly the mindset of a prospective student exploring programs. We treat it as an experimental test line, not a core channel: a small, measured budget with conversion tracking wired in from day one, scaled only if it produces inquiries at a defensible cost.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         SEO / AEO readiness — As search shifts toward AI answers, we advise on the content and structure that get NECC programs surfaced in AI-generated results — the SEO/AEO plus the RFP noted. Strong answer-engine positioning also compounds the value of any future ChatGPT Ads spend.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:line="276" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="1340" w:hanging="440"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">•         First-mover advantage — These surfaces are cheaper and less crowded today than they will be in a year. Establishing NECC’s presence now is a low-cost hedge that pays off as the channels mature.
<w:tblPr><w:tblStyle w:val="Table20"/><w:tblW w:w="9350.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:jc w:val="left"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:insideH w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/><w:insideV w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="4" w:val="single"/></w:tblBorders><w:tblLayout w:type="fixed"/><w:tblLook w:val="0400"/></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="1898"/><w:gridCol w:w="7452"/><w:tblGridChange w:id="0"><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="1898"/><w:gridCol w:w="7452"/></w:tblGrid></w:tblGridChange></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:trPr><w:cantSplit w:val="1"/><w:tblHeader w:val="0"/></w:trPr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="e63412" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="center"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="000001F9"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="ffffff"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">AI GOVERNANCE
<w:tcPr><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="000001FA"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">We use AI to optimize media, never to replace human judgment on messaging that affects students. All audience modeling respects platform policy and student-privacy expectations, and a human reviews every creative and targeting decision before it goes live.
Creative Process & Ad Design Samples
Northern Essex handles creative in-house, so our role is to make your team's work perform. We bring the briefs, the format specs, and the performance data that tell you which creative to make more of.
How We Collaborate With Your In-House Team
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Creative briefs — We supply channel-specific briefs with audience, message, and format guidance so your designers build for performance.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Ad copy — We develop written copy variants for A/B testing: search headlines, social body copy, CTA variations. NECC's marketing team reviews and approves before trafficking. Turnaround works best on a 5–7 business day review cycle, which we build into the campaign calendar.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Format & spec sheets — Exact dimensions and asset counts per platform, so nothing is reworked after the fact.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="0" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Performance-based rotation — We report which creative converts and recommend what to refresh, retire, or scale.
<w:tab w:val="left" w:leader="none" w:pos="864"/></w:tabs><w:spacing w:after="80" w:lineRule="auto"/><w:ind w:left="720" w:hanging="360"/><w:rPr><w:u w:val="none"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Accessibility — We advise on WCAG-aligned creative — contrast, captions, and legibility — so ads reach every prospective student.

https://shorturl.at/JmBVU
<w:pgSz w:h="15840" w:w="12240" w:orient="portrait"/><w:pgMar w:bottom="1440" w:top="1440" w:left="1440" w:right="1440" w:header="720" w:footer="720"/></w:sectPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Northern Essex's brand is handled in-house. Our role is to extend it across digital platforms, not reinvent it. We adapt your existing assets into channel-ready executions — display banners, social cutdowns, and retargeting formats — ensuring every impression reinforces NECC's identity while your team maintains creative control.
Specific Deliverables & Project Timeline
The contract runs August 1, 2026 through June 30, 2027 — eleven months across all four of your enrollment cycles. Day one is planned: by the end of the first month, accounts are connected, tracking is validated, fall campaigns are live, and your dashboard is open. From there, the work settles into a predictable rhythm of recurring deliverables layered over season-specific pushes.
What you can count on every month, regardless of season:
Live dashboard access — near real-time visibility into spend, pacing, traffic, and conversions, available to your team 24/7.
Monthly performance report — written analysis delivered within 5 business days of month-end, with results tied to inquiries and applications, not vanity metrics.
Monthly strategy call — a working review of what moved, what we changed, and what we recommend next, at whatever cadence NECC prefers.
Continuous optimization — daily pacing and bid management plus a weekly optimization pass, so the budget is always working toward what converts.
Detailed monthly invoicing — a clear breakout of working media versus management fee, with the PO number, sent to the accounts payable addresses in your RFP.
Against that steady baseline, the calendar below maps the season-specific deliverables — campaign launches, creative refreshes, and conversion pushes — to each of your fall, winter, spring, and summer enrollment cycles.
A couple of notes:
The recurring-deliverables block does double duty: it directly answers the RFP's reporting questions (dashboard access, monthly reporting cadence, strategy calls) inside the timeline section, reinforcing what you said earlier in the proposal rather than leaving the committee to flip back.
The "5 business days of month-end" and "daily pacing / weekly pass" specifics match what's already in your Account Team and Optimization sections, so the proposal stays internally consistent — a committee that cross-checks won't find a contradiction.

A full year of conversion data sharpens audience targeting, lowers cost per application, and lets us forecast each enrollment cycle with real NECC history behind it.
Your RFP requires a clear breakout of paid advertising costs versus agency management fees against a Year-One budget of $130,000 to $140,000. Below is that breakout at the midpoint, with a management fee of 15% of total budget. All figures are illustrative at the $135,000 midpoint and final terms are confirmed at award. NECC's enrollment goal of 6,000 credit students for FY2026-2027 puts the investment in context: at the $135,000 midpoint, that is approximately $135 per net new student at goal — efficient against community college industry benchmarks.

Appendix: Case Studies
Four case studies matched to Northern Essex's profile: a Hispanic Serving Institution, two community colleges judged on cost-efficient lead generation, and large public universities recruiting through measurable paid media. Each reflects the outcomes this RFP names — qualified inquiries, application conversion, and enrollment growth — delivered through the full channel mix, inside a defined service area.
Community College of Aurora (Aurora, CO)
Community College / Hispanic Serving Institution — our closest audience match to Northern Essex.

THE CHALLENGE
The Community College of Aurora, a federally designated Hispanic Serving Institution in the Denver metro, competes for prospective students against four-year transfer destinations and nearby community colleges — many with larger recruitment budgets. CCA needed to convert active demand into measurable inquiries and enrolled students while reaching a diverse audience of first-year students, adult learners, transfer-seekers, and first-generation populations.
As New England's first federally designated HSI, Northern Essex recruits the same audience profile — a roughly 49% Latino, heavily first-generation student body — inside a defined service territory. The parallel is direct.
OUR APPROACH
We built a full-funnel program across the channels this RFP calls for, anchored in CCA's service area and tuned continuously against conversion data in GA4. The channels worked together: search captured high-intent demand, social and programmatic built awareness among adult learners and families, and geofencing concentrated spend where prospects actually were.
Geography-first targeting — Campaigns built around CCA's service area, with bilingual and equity-aligned creative for a Hispanic-Serving audience.
Full channel mix — Google Ads, Meta, programmatic display, site retargeting, and geofencing working together across the funnel — not a single tactic carrying the program.
Continuous optimization — Daily search-term review, bid management, and audience refinement, with budget reallocated toward the channels producing the lowest cost per qualified inquiry.

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000291"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Mid-campaign data showed Meta outproducing display for adult-learner inquiries. We reallocated budget into Meta within the same month, contributing to 759 Meta-sourced leads in a 30-day window. The lesson: we move money toward what is converting, fast — instead of waiting for the next reporting cycle.

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000294"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">CCA shares Northern Essex's defining feature — an HSI serving a first-generation, Latino, and adult-learner audience — and the result came from the full channel mix NECC's RFP asks for, not a single tactic. The same geography-first, omnichannel approach we deployed at the Community College of Aurora is the foundation of our recommendation for Northern Essex.
Asheville-Buncombe Technical Community College (Asheville, NC)
Community College / Workforce — a lean-team, defined-budget analog to Northern Essex. Live, contactable reference.

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="dddddd" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="dddddd" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="ffffff" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="60.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="60.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="000002A3"><w:pPr><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="17"/><w:szCs w:val="17"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="17"/><w:szCs w:val="17"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">90-day flight
THE CHALLENGE
AB Tech, a North Carolina community college, needed a steady, affordable flow of qualified inquiries for its workforce and degree programs — competing for attention against larger regional institutions with bigger advertising budgets. Its lean marketing team did not have the internal capacity to build, monitor, and optimize campaigns day to day.
The mandate mirrors Northern Essex closely: a community college with a defined budget where every inquiry has to come in at a defensible cost.
OUR APPROACH
We ran an omnichannel program inside the college's own ad accounts, with conversion tracking validated in GA4 before a dollar went live. We concentrated budget on high-intent program audiences across paid search, paid social, and programmatic display, and protected efficiency with disciplined optimization from day one.
Omnichannel build — Paid search, paid social, and programmatic display, structured by program so spend tracked to the inquiries that mattered.
Daily optimization — Bid adjustments, search-term mining, and negative-keyword additions every day — not monthly — driving cost per lead down as the campaign matured.
Conversion tracking — GA4 and event tracking from launch, so every lead was attributable to a campaign, channel, and cost — the same integration NECC requires.
THE RESULTS

THE CHALLENGE
Kennesaw State, one of the largest public universities in Georgia, needed a sustained, cost-efficient flow of undergraduate inquiries in a highly competitive metro-Atlanta recruitment market. This was not a one-campaign engagement — KSU needed a partner who could run paid search consistently, season after season, and keep driving cost down as the program matured.
For Northern Essex, the relevant proof here is consistency: the same accountability and steady inquiry generation across many recruitment cycles that NECC needs across its fall, winter, spring, and summer terms.
OUR APPROACH
We have managed Kennesaw State's undergraduate paid search since August 2019 — a partnership now in its sixth-plus year, which is itself the strongest proof of accountability and consistency. The program runs on high-intent Google Ads search, structured by program intent and continuously optimized against cost per lead, with conversion tracking carrying inquiries through the funnel.
High-intent search — Google Ads campaigns built around undergraduate program and degree-intent keywords, capturing students at the moment they are actively searching.
Daily optimization — Ongoing keyword, bid, and negative-keyword management holding undergraduate cost per lead under $8 while sustaining a 13.24% click-through rate.
Conversion-rate focus — Search traffic matched to conversion-ready destinations, producing a 12.32% lead conversion rate.
The Results
Figures reflect the undergraduate program over a recent six-month reporting period (December 2025 – June 2026).

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="dddddd" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="dddddd" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="ffffff" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="60.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="60.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="100.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="00000301"><w:pPr><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="17"/><w:szCs w:val="17"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:sz w:val="17"/><w:szCs w:val="17"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Four-state footprint
THE CHALLENGE
Utah State needed to capture program search demand efficiently across a broad, multi-state geography. The engagement spanned multiple academic programs across a four-state footprint, which meant search budget had to work hard across markets with very different levels of competition and cost — the same market-by-market efficiency challenge Northern Essex faces across its Massachusetts and New Hampshire territory.
OUR APPROACH
We built and managed paid search as the lead-generation engine, with keyword and geographic strategy tuned to a large public institution's program mix. Campaigns were structured by program and region so budget followed demand, and conversion tracking fed monthly reporting against efficiency KPIs — the same reporting discipline NECC is asking for.
Geo-targeted search — Campaigns segmented across a four-state footprint so bids and budget adapted to each market's search competition and cost — directly relevant to NECC's MA + NH border-zip targeting.
Program-level structure — Keyword groups organized by degree program, keeping spend and cost per lead measurable at the program level, not just the account level.
Monthly efficiency reporting — Performance reported against blended cost per lead and conversion KPIs every month — the cadence NECC specified.

<w:tblPr><w:tblStyle w:val="Table40"/><w:tblW w:w="8895.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:jc w:val="left"/><w:tblBorders><w:top w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:right w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:insideH w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:insideV w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/></w:tblBorders><w:tblLayout w:type="fixed"/><w:tblLook w:val="0600"/></w:tblPr><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="2145"/><w:gridCol w:w="6750"/><w:tblGridChange w:id="0"><w:tblGrid><w:gridCol w:w="2145"/><w:gridCol w:w="6750"/></w:tblGrid></w:tblGridChange></w:tblGrid><w:tr><w:trPr><w:cantSplit w:val="0"/><w:trHeight w:val="1425" w:hRule="atLeast"/><w:tblHeader w:val="0"/></w:trPr><w:tc><w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="e63412" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000031B"><w:pPr><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="ffffff"/></w:rPr></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:b w:val="1"/><w:bCs w:val="1"/><w:color w:val="ffffff"/><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">OPTIMIZATION IN ACTION
<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000031C"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Holding a blended cost per lead across a four-state footprint takes constant rebalancing — shifting budget away from high-cost markets toward the programs and regions converting most efficiently. That market-by-market discipline is exactly how we would manage NECC's spend across its priority towns and NH border zips.

<w:tcPr><w:tcBorders><w:top w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:left w:color="000000" w:space="0" w:sz="0" w:val="nil"/><w:bottom w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/><w:right w:color="e63412" w:space="0" w:sz="6" w:val="single"/></w:tcBorders><w:shd w:fill="fff8e1" w:val="clear"/><w:tcMar><w:top w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:left w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:bottom w:w="120.0" w:type="dxa"/><w:right w:w="140.0" w:type="dxa"/></w:tcMar><w:vAlign w:val="top"/></w:tcPr><w:p w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidP="00000000" w:rsidRDefault="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000" w14:paraId="0000031F"><w:pPr><w:rPr/></w:pPr><w:r w:rsidDel="00000000" w:rsidR="00000000" w:rsidRPr="00000000"><w:rPr><w:rtl w:val="0"/></w:rPr><w:t xml:space="preserve">Utah State shows we can run program-level paid search across varied markets and report it on the monthly efficiency KPIs NECC specified. The same segmented, market-by-market model is the one we would bring to Northern Essex's defined service territory on day one.
A Note on Seasonal & Event Campaigns
Many of our higher education partners operate the way Northern Essex does: ongoing seasonal enrollment campaigns, with event- and program-specific pushes activated throughout the year. We are structured for exactly this. Each seasonal flight and event campaign receives its own brief, audience build, and end-of-campaign report — without disrupting the primary optimization cadence. There is no setup fee for adding an event or program campaign within an active contract.
Each case study above reflects this same model: sustained partnership outcomes across inquiries, applications, efficient budget use, and year-over-year improvement — not single-campaign results.
Appendix: References
Three contactable references, all higher education clients.
